Simulated and real-world bending angles show comparable response. These methods provide a repeatable and robust fabrication procedure, validated across two operators and 24 successful fabrications, along with benchmark simulations and experimental testing. These benchmarks will enable more widespread adoption of soft robotics.

</details>


### [70] [VTAP Gripper: Synergizing Fingertip Sensing and a Visuo-Tactile Active Palm for Dexterous In-Hand Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15448)
*Yuhao Zhou,Sheeraz Athar,Zhixian Hu,Binghao Huang,Yunzhu Li,Juan Wachs,Yu She*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种触觉反应型夹爪，通过结合视觉-触觉主动手掌（VTAP）和配备触觉阵列传感器的柔性可重构手指，利用结构化手指-手掌协同和多模态感知，实现了鲁棒的抓取和精细操作。同时提出了一种分阶段、手势条件化的重定向框架用于灵巧遥操作，在YCB物体抓取、注射器操作、微小物体分拣（3mm）和视觉-触觉插孔插入等任务中验证了高性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有灵巧操作方案通常依赖高自由度仿人设计，但高自由度带来机械复杂性、控制难度和成本问题。本文旨在探索是否可以通过手指-手掌协同和多模态感知以较低的自由度实现同等甚至更优的操作性能，并解决异构三指结构与人类手部运动之间的具身差异问题。

Method: 1) 设计了VTAP夹爪，包含一个可主动驱动的双模态手掌（结合长距离视觉定位和接触式触觉反馈）和三个配备触觉阵列传感器的柔性可重构手指；2) 提出分阶段、手势条件化的重定向框架，将人类手部运动映射到异构三指结构，实现灵巧遥操作；3) 系统通过手指-手掌协同和视觉-触觉多模态感知完成抓取和操作。

Result: 在多个挑战性任务中验证了系统性能：YCB物体和易碎物体的反应式抓取、注射器在手中的重新定向和柱塞驱动、低至3mm直径的聚类物体分离、以及视觉-触觉的销孔插入任务。结果表明，通过协调的手指-手掌交互和多模态感知，可以在不依赖高自由度仿人设计的情况下实现高操作性能。

Conclusion: VTAP夹爪及其重定向框架为灵巧夹爪设计、操作和接触丰富的数据采集提供了一个实用的参考架构，有助于支持基于学习的操作方法。手指-手掌协同和多模态感知是替代高自由度仿人设计的有效途径。

Abstract: This paper presents a tactile-reactive gripper that integrates a Visuo-Tactile Active Palm (VTAP) and compliant, reconfigurable fingers equipped with tactile array sensors. The design exploits structured finger-palm synergy and multi-modal perception to achieve both robust grasping and fine manipulation. The actuated bi-modal palm seamlessly combines long-range visual localization with contact-rich tactile feedback, substantially extending the system's manipulation capability. To bridge the embodiment gap between human hand motion and the heterogeneous three-finger structure, we further propose a staged, gesture-conditioned retargeting framework for dexterous teleoperation. Extensive experiments validate the system across a range of challenging tasks: reactive grasping of YCB and fragile objects, in-hand syringe reorientation and plunger actuation, singulation of clustered objects down to 3 mm in diameter, and vision-tactile peg-in-hole insertion. Results demonstrate that high manipulation performance can be achieved through coordinated finger-palm interaction and multi-modal sensing, without resorting to high degrees of freedom anthropomorphic designs. The VTAP gripper and its retargeting framework offer a practical reference architecture for dexterous gripper design, manipulation, and contact-rich data collection in support of learning-based approaches. Project webpage: https://yuhochau.github.io/vtap/.

</details>


### [71] [Risk-Aware Preference Learning for Stochastic Outcomes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15483)
*Yi-Shiuan Tung,Yuni Wu,Wei Jiang,Alessandro Roncone,Bradley Hayes*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文研究了在社交机器人导航中，当人类表现出风险敏感性（如损失厌恶、低估罕见负面事件）时，使用累积前景理论（CPT）替代传统期望效用理论（EU）来学习奖励函数，能显著降低遗憾值，表明建模人类风险敏感性对偏好学习至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有奖励学习方法假设人类使用期望效用（EU）评估不确定结果，但行为证据表明人类具有系统性的风险敏感性（如高估罕见负事件、损失厌恶）。这种错误假设在安全关键的社交机器人导航场景中可能导致严重后果，因此需要研究如何更好地建模人类实际决策行为。

Method: 在Bradley-Terry偏好学习框架下，分别使用期望效用理论（EU）和累积前景理论（CPT）两种决策模型来学习奖励函数，并通过对比实验评估两种方法在风险敏感用户生成的偏好数据上的表现。

Result: 当偏好数据由风险敏感用户生成时，基于CPT的奖励学习方法相比基于EU的方法获得了显著更低的遗憾值（regret）。

Conclusion: 在从随机机器人行为结果的偏好中学习奖励时，建模人类风险敏感性非常重要，忽视这一特性会导致次优的奖励函数和更高的决策遗憾。

Abstract: Learning reward functions from human preferences is a widely used approach for aligning robot behavior with user expectations in human-robot interaction. Most existing approaches assume that humans evaluate uncertain outcomes using expected utility (EU), aggregating outcome utilities linearly with their probabilities. However, behavioral evidence shows that humans are systematically risk-sensitive, overweighting rare negative events and exhibiting loss aversion. We study the consequences of this mismatch in social robot navigation, where safety-critical outcomes (e.g., collisions) are rare but highly consequential. We compare EU with Cumulative Prospect Theory (CPT), a nonlinear model of human decision-making, within a Bradley-Terry preference learning framework. Our preliminary experiments show that when preferences are generated by risk-sensitive users, CPT-based learners recover reward functions with substantially lower regret compared to EU-based learners. Our results highlight the importance of modeling human risk sensitivity when learning rewards from preferences over stochastic robot outcomes.

</details>


### [72] [Environment Design for Reliable Shared Autonomy with Probabilistic Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15487)
*Yi-Shiuan Tung,Himanshu Gupta,Gyanig Kumar,Heyang Huang,Bradley Hayes,Alessandro Roncone*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出通过优化物理工作空间布局（物体摆放）来提升共享自主系统中人类意图推断的可靠性，减少噪声输入下的目标歧义，并通过仿真和真实实验验证了效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有共享自主系统多关注在固定环境下的意图推断，忽略了工作空间设计本身对推断难度的影响。物理物体的排列会直接影响噪声用户输入下候选目标的可分离性，因此有必要将环境设计作为优化变量来提升系统性能。

Method: 将工作空间布局形式化为一个优化问题，在有界噪声模型下推导出概率正确性保证。通过仿真实验（多种桌面场景）对比优化布局与基准布局，并在真实共享自主系统中集成该推断框架进行验证。

Result: 优化后的布局相比基准布局显著提升了目标推断的可靠性，减少了歧义。真实系统集成验证了该方法的可行性和有效性。

Conclusion: 环境设计是改进共享自主系统的一个互补且有效的维度，通过合理布置物体可降低意图推断难度，值得在系统设计中加以利用。

Abstract: Shared autonomy enables humans and robots to collaboratively perform tasks by combining human input with autonomous assistance. Most prior work focuses on improving intent inference under a fixed environment, overlooking how workspace design itself affects inference difficulty. We observe that the physical arrangement of objects directly influences the separability of candidate goals under noisy user inputs. We formulate workspace design as an optimization problem and derive a probabilistic correctness guarantee under a bounded noise model. Through simulation experiments across multiple tabletop scenarios, we show that optimized layouts improve goal inference reliability and reduce ambiguity compared to baseline arrangements. We further demonstrate a real-world shared autonomy system that integrates the proposed inference framework. This highlights the role of environment design as a complementary axis for improving shared autonomy systems.

</details>


### [73] [Multi-Objective Kinodynamic Motion Planning with Asymptotic Pareto Optimality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15508)
*Yusif Razzaq,Anne Theurkauf,Nisar Ahmed,Morteza Lahijanian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对运动学约束下的多目标运动规划问题，提出了统一算法框架，包含lexSST（字典序优化）、coSST（约束优化）和poSST（帕累托前沿近似）三种算法，并提供了完备性与最优性的理论保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有成本标量化方法无法在连续域系统中提供正确性保证，因而需要新的多目标运动规划算法。

Method: 基于Stable Sparse-RRT (SST)算法，将每个见证邻域中维护的单一代表节点替换为局部帕累托最优代表节点集，从而衍生出三种算法：lexSST、coSST和poSST。

Result: 提供了所提算法的完备性和最优性理论证明，并通过广泛的实证研究验证了算法的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的统一框架能有效解决运动学约束下多目标运动规划的三大类问题，具备理论保证和实践效果。

Abstract: In this paper, we address the challenge of multi-objective motion planning for systems under kinodynamic constraints. We consider three problem classes: (i) lexicographic optimization, in which objectives are minimized according to a strict priority ordering, (ii) constrained optimization, in which a primary objective is minimized subject to bounds on the remaining costs, and (iii) Pareto front optimization, in which the goal is to approximate the full set of optimal trade-offs among competing objectives. We first show that established cost scalarization methods for multi-objective problems cannot be extended to continuous-domain systems with correctness guarantees. Then, we propose a unified algorithmic framework built upon the Stable Sparse-RRT (SST) algorithm, in which the single representative maintained at each witness neighborhood is replaced by a representative set of locally Pareto-optimal nodes. This structure gives rise to three distinct algorithms: lexSST for lexicographic minimization, coSST for constrained optimization, and poSST for Pareto-front approximation. We provide theoretical guarantees for the completeness and optimality of our algorithms and demonstrate their effectiveness through extensive empirical evaluations.

</details>


### [74] [PACE: Persona Adaptation through Conversational Elicitation in Human-Robot Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15579)
*Peizhen Li,Longbing Cao,Megani Rajendran,Timothy Liu,Aik Beng Ng,Simon See*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出PACE框架，通过交互式对话引导为用户生成结构化、可适配的个性身份，并在Ameca人形机器人上实现多模态表达，相比静态基线显著提升用户信任、拟人化感知和交互质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法依赖静态、硬编码的机器人身份，缺乏对用户个体场景的适应性，无法支持自然、可信的人机交互。

Method: 提出PACE框架，包括交互式个性引导管线（通过用户问答动态合成心理学的身份）、个性提示词编译阶段（生成多维度结构化提示词）以及具身系统集成（将结构化规范转化为多模态人形行为）。

Result: 通过实证HRI评估，动态生成个性相比静态基线在用户信任、拟人化感知、个性一致性、个人相关性和交互质量上均有提升。

Conclusion: PACE为在具身人形助手中部署个性化、可交互且可靠的机器人身份提供了可扩展路径。

Abstract: Equipping humanoid robots with coherent and adaptable personas is crucial for fostering natural, engaging, and trustworthy human-robot interaction (HRI). However, existing approaches often rely on static, hard-coded identities that lack the flexibility to adapt to individual user contexts. In this paper, we present PACE (Persona Adaptation through Conversational Elicitation), a novel framework for the interactive generation and deployment of structured personas on the Ameca humanoid robot. Our system introduces an Interactive Persona Elicitation Pipeline, enabling the robot to dynamically synthesize a tailored, psychologically grounded identity through user Q&A. This elicitation process feeds into a persona prompt compilation phase, generating a structured persona prompt built upon multi-perspective dimensions. We detail the Embodied System Integration required to translate this structured specification into expressive, multimodal humanoid behaviors. Through a comprehensive empirical HRI evaluation, we assess the impact of dynamically generated personas on user trust, perceived anthropomorphism, persona consistency, personal relevance, and interaction quality compared to a generic baseline. These contributions establish a scalable pathway for deploying personalized, interactive, and reliable identities in embodied humanoid assistants. Video demo is available at: https://lipzh5.github.io/PACE/

</details>


### [75] [A Model-Based Decoupling Strategy for Proprioception and Contact Sensing in an Architected Soft Manipulator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15582)
*Francesco Stella,Annan Zhang,Cosimo Della Santina,Josie Hughes,Daniela Rus*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于模型的方法，通过流体压力传感器实现软体连续机器人的本体感知和接触检测，在单段结构中达到0.11±0.02的相对弯曲误差和97%的接触检测率，并集成了八段结构展示了全臂触觉示教、力调节和物体重建等应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软体连续机器人需要嵌入式感知实现本体感知和接触检测，但在稀疏、高度可变形架构结构中集成传感器极具挑战性。

Method: 提出Innervated Trimmed Helicoid (ITH)结构，每段包含六个呈锯齿形布线的气路通道；采用分段常曲率模型将压力映射为形状，利用Huber回归识别残差异常通道以检测外部接触，形成过定方程组解耦本体感知与接触信号。

Result: 单段ITH：相对弯曲误差0.11±0.02，接触检测率97%（178次试验）；集成八段构成Air-Helix软体连续操作臂，实现全臂触觉示教、导纳控制力调节和触觉物体重建。

Conclusion: 局部流体神经支配结合基于模型的冗余解析是实现架构化软体机器人本体感知与接触检测同步的实用路径。

Abstract: Soft continuum robots require embedded sensing for proprioception and contact detection, yet integrating sensors into sparse, highly deformable architected structures remains challenging. We present a model-based strategy that decouples proprioceptive and contact signals from a common set of fluidic pressure sensors embedded in a soft architected segment. Each segment of the Innervated Trimmed Helicoid (ITH) contains six air channels routed in a localized zigzag pattern along the circumference. With only three principal kinematic degrees of freedom (axial compression, bending in x, bending in y), the six pressure readings form an overdetermined system. A piecewise constant curvature model maps pressures to shape, and Huber regression identifies outlier channels whose residuals indicate external contact. On a single ITH segment, this approach achieves proprioceptive shape estimation with a relative bending error of 0.11 +/- 0.02 and a contact detection rate of 97% across 178 trials. We integrate eight ITH segments into Air-Helix, a tendon-driven soft continuum manipulator, and present exploratory whole-arm demonstrations that include tactile teaching by demonstration, admittance-controlled force regulation, and tactile object reconstruction. The results suggest that localized fluidic innervation combined with model-based redundancy resolution is a practical path toward concurrent proprioception and contact sensing in architected soft robots.

</details>


### [76] [MemoGuard: An Adaptive Runtime for Guarding Against Memory Traps in Communication-Limited Robot Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15589)
*Rajat Bhattacharjya,Hyeonjong Ju,Sing-Yao Wu,Eli Bozorgzadeh,Nikil Dutt*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出MemoGuard，一种轻量级自适应运行时系统，通过在重用前验证情景记忆的拓扑、资源和结果契约，在灾难巡检等通信受限场景中平衡机器人的安全性与效率。在走廊巡检模拟器中，相比纯相似性重用法，电池安全违规减少76.6%；相比始终调用本地推理，回退调用减少21.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通信受限机器人在关键任务场景中需要可靠机载决策，但情景记忆重用时高相似度的检索动作可能因拓扑变化、电池余量不足或先前结果不可靠而执行无效（即"记忆陷阱"），导致安全-效率权衡困境：纯相似性重用低成本但不安全，始终本地推理安全但高计算能耗成本。

Method: 提出MemoGuard，一种轻量级自适应运行时系统，在重用情景记忆前，针对拓扑契约、资源契约和结果契约三个维度进行有效性验证，仅在验证失败时回退到本地推理（如llama3.2:3b）。

Result: 在基于图的走廊巡检模拟器中，相比top-1纯相似性重用，电池安全违规减少76.6%；相比始终本地推理，回退调用减少21.4%。在NVIDIA Jetson AGX Xavier上，每次试验可节省3.67秒和36.97焦耳的推理开销。

Conclusion: MemoGuard通过轻量级契约验证有效解决了情景记忆重用中的安全-效率权衡问题，在减少安全违规的同时降低了计算和能量开销，代码已开源。

Abstract: Communication-limited robots in mission-critical scenarios such as disaster inspection and search-and-rescue must make reliable onboard decisions without access to remote operators or high-capacity reasoning services. Episodic memory reuse is an attractive low-cost fallback, but retrieval similarity does not guarantee execution validity, i.e., a retrieved action may match the current context yet be unsafe due to changed topology, insufficient battery margin, or unreliable prior outcomes. We call such high-similarity but execution-invalid episodes memory traps. This creates a safety-efficiency design space where similarity only reuse minimizes fallback cost but can be unsafe, while always invoking local reasoning improves safety at high computational and energy cost. This paper presents MemoGuard, a lightweight adaptive runtime that validates episodic memories against topology, resource, and outcome contracts before reuse, invoking fallback only when validation fails. In a graph-based corridor-inspection simulator, MemoGuard reduces battery safety violations by 76.6% over similarity-only top-1 reuse while reducing fallback calls by 21.4% over always reasoning. On an NVIDIA Jetson AGX Xavier with local llama3.2:3b fallback reasoning, this corresponds to 3.67 s and 36.97 J of avoided fallback-reasoning overhead per trial. We open-source MemoGuard at https://github.com/hetheiin/memoguard.

</details>


### [77] [AEGIS: Assay-Aware Protocol Validation and Runtime Monitoring for Open-Source Liquid Handling Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15620)
*Priyanka V. Setty,Arvind Ramanathan,Ian Foster,Rick Stevens*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AEGIS是一个双层守护系统，为开源液体处理器Opentrons OT-2提供实验前基于规则的协议验证（第一层，F1=0.97）和运行时视觉监控（第二层，AP=0.89），统一了飞行前检测和运行中故障检测，开源且无需付费API。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低成本液体处理器（如Opentrons OT-2）缺乏高端系统（Hamilton/Tecan）的压力监测功能，通常在开环状态下运行，导致两类故障无法被检测：语法正确但违反实验特定不变量的协议，以及运行时的物理执行故障（如部分分配、气泡、丢失吸头）。现有系统缺乏统一的解决方案。

Method: AEGIS包含两层：（1）第一层将策划的机器可读实验规则库与LLM推理相结合，分析OT-2 Python代码，在五个实验系列24个协议基准上达到调整F1=0.97；（2）第二层使用PCA世界模型拟合YOLO裁剪的四帧移液轨迹，在无泄漏的留一板评估中达到平均精度0.89、F1=0.71；采用级联分诊策略，VLM成本约为每板$1.63。

Result: 第一层在24协议基准上F1=0.97，超越规则-only和LLM-only消融实验；第二层在物理OT-2现场演示中确定性捕获无吸头故障，部分分配召回率提升至5/5；小移液器(p20)分辨率极限F1=0.47；VLM成本从$10.33降至$1.63/板。

Conclusion: AEGIS是首个为开源液体处理器统一飞行前实验感知验证与运行时视觉监控的系统，在低成本平台上实现了对协议逻辑错误和物理执行故障的全面检测，开源可用。

Abstract: Self-driving laboratories increasingly rely on low-cost liquid handlers such as the Opentrons OT-2, which ship without the pressure-based aspiration monitoring of Hamilton or Tecan systems and are typically run open-loop. Two failure modes go undetected: protocols that are syntactically valid but violate assay-specific invariants (e.g., tip reuse between a PCR template and a no-template control), and physical execution failures (partial dispense, air bubbles, missing tips) at runtime. We present AEGIS, a two-layer guardian for both. Layer 1 pairs a curated machine-readable assay rule database with an LLM that reasons over OT-2 Python code, reaching an adjusted F1 of 0.97 on a 24-protocol benchmark across five assay families and beating rules-only and LLM-only ablations across five backends; a free open-weight model ties the best proprietary one, so no paid API is required. Layer 2 fits a PCA world model to YOLO-cropped four-frame pipette trajectories; under a leakage-free leave-one-plate-out evaluation it reaches average precision 0.89 and operating-point F1 0.71 (AUROC 0.80), a deployment-faithful number that matches the live demonstration, and we characterize the small-pipette (p20) resolution limit (F1 0.47). A live demonstration on a physical OT-2 (five replicates per condition) catches planted no-tip failures deterministically and partial dispense on coloured dyes, with an always-VLM self-vote gate lifting partial-dispense recall to 5/5; transparent water is a principled limit of any front-view-only monitor, which AEGIS surfaces as low-confidence VLM reasoning rather than a wrong verdict. Cascade triage holds VLM cost near $1.63 per plate versus $10.33 for an always-VLM baseline. AEGIS is open source and, to our knowledge, the first system to unify pre-flight assay-aware validation with runtime visual monitoring for an open-source liquid handler.

</details>


### [78] [Think at 5 Hz, Act at 20 Hz: Asynchronous Fast-Slow Vision-Language-Action Inference for Closed-Loop Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15621)
*Yun Li,Jiachen Gong,Simon Thompson,Ehsan Javanmardi,Qunli Zhang,Zifan Zeng,Shiming Liu,Peng Wang,Zixuan Guo,Manabu Tsukada*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种快慢架构（fast-slow architecture），将7B视觉语言模型作为慢系统（低频处理导航指令和视觉历史），轻量动作专家作为快系统（每仿真 tick 关注缓存和当前帧回归路径点），通过随机过期训练对齐异步执行，在CARLA LangAuto-Short路线上，每50ms产生新鲜控制指令，路线完成率从37.0提升至94.0，红灯违规减少三分之一，零样本迁移至未见城镇仍保持84-94%完成率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型驱动的端到端驾驶存在推理延迟与车辆控制率不匹配的问题。现有闭环智能体通过跳帧（alternate ticks）并重复上一指令来隐藏此差距，导致一半控制输出忽略最新观测，影响安全性和性能。

Method: 1) 快慢架构：冻结7B视觉语言骨干作为慢系统，低频处理导航指令和视觉历史，暴露每层KV缓存作为场景表征；轻量动作专家作为快系统，每仿真tick关注该缓存和当前帧，单次前向回归路径点。2) 随机过期训练：在训练时引入随机滞后的KV缓存，使专家适应部署时的异步执行。3) 帧跳过消融实验分离专家能力和每tick新鲜度的影响。

Result: 在CARLA LangAuto-Short路线上，每50ms控制tick产生新鲜指令，路线完成率从37.0提升至94.0；消融显示专家自身提升驾驶分数，而每tick新鲜度将完成率从82.1提升至94.0并减少三分之一红灯违规；零样本迁移至两个未见城镇保持84-94%完成率（基线31-41%）；开放循环路径点误差降低近四倍，每tick模型成本32ms（单消费级GPU，与历史长度无关）。

Conclusion: 快慢架构有效解决LLM推理延迟与控制率不匹配问题，通过分离慢系统（认知/规划）和快系统（反应/控制）实现每tick新鲜控制，随机过期训练保证了异步执行的对齐，大幅提升驾驶性能和安全性，且具有良好的零样本泛化能力。

Abstract: Large language models bring instruction following and scene reasoning to end-to-end driving, but their inference latency collides with the control rate a vehicle requires. Existing closed-loop agents hide this gap by invoking the model on alternate simulation ticks and replaying the previous command in between, so half of all control outputs ignore the newest observations. We present a fast-slow architecture that removes this compromise. A frozen 7B vision-language backbone acts as the slow system, digesting navigation instructions and visual history at low frequency while exposing its per-layer key-value cache as a standing representation of the scene. A lightweight action expert acts as the fast system, attending to this cache and to the current camera frame at every simulation tick to regress waypoints in a single forward pass. Since the cache lags behind the world at deployment, we train the expert under randomized staleness, aligning training with asynchronous execution. On LangAuto-Short routes in CARLA, our system produces fresh control at every 50 ms simulation tick and lifts route completion from 37.0 to 94.0 over the frame-skipping baseline. A frame-skip ablation with the same expert separates the two factors at work: the expert raises the driving score on its own, while per-tick freshness raises completion from 82.1 to 94.0 and cuts red-light violations by a third. Trained on a single town, the expert transfers zero-shot to two unseen towns, holding 84-94% route completion where the baseline reaches 31-41%. It reduces open-loop waypoint error by nearly a factor of four compared to the backbone's own action head, at a per-tick model cost of 32 ms that is independent of history length on a single consumer GPU.

</details>


### [79] [Scalable Open-Source Visuotactile Sensor for 6-Axis Contact Wrench Estimation in Tensegrity Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15633)
*Wenzhe Tong,Jonathan Mi,Xili Yi,Nima Fazeli,Xiaonan Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种可扩展的开源视触觉传感系统，用于张拉整体机器人，能够实现六轴力矩估计和接触检测，实验验证了其准确性和实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 张拉整体机器人缺乏实用、低成本且可重复的触觉反馈方案，现有触觉传感系统难以兼顾轻量化、耐久性和大规模部署的需求。

Method: 设计了一种端盖传感器，包含弹性体外壳、3D打印热塑性聚氨酯（TPU）接口和嵌入式摄像头与LED照明环的刚性基座；提出了一种新型螺旋填充粘合技术，无需粘合剂即可形成耐用的弹性体-TPU界面；通过触觉到力矩的神经网络将剪切矢量场映射为六维力和力矩测量值。

Result: 静态验证数据均方误差（MSE）为0.1531，具备动态运动下的域外泛化能力；在12 kg张拉整体机器人上全系统集成验证了可靠的地面接触识别能力。

Conclusion: 该系统显著提升了张拉整体机器人触觉反馈的实用性，提供了一条低成本、可重复、物理可解释的路径，用于实现接触感知的本体感知和状态估计。

Abstract: This paper presents a scalable, open-source visuotactile sensing system for tensegrity robots that enables six-axis wrench estimation and contact detection. The proposed endcap sensor integrates an elastomeric shell, a 3D-printed thermoplastic polyurethane (TPU) interface, and a rigid base housing an embedded camera and LED illumination ring. A novel gyroid-infill bonding technique is introduced to form a durable elastomer-TPU interface without adhesives, yielding a lightweight and modular design compatible with large-scale tensegrity structures. A tactile-to-wrench neural network maps shear vector fields to six-dimensional force and torque measurements. Experimental results demonstrate accurate and stable wrench estimation with a mean squared error (MSE) of 0.1531 on static validation data and out-of-domain generalization under dynamic motion. Furthermore, full-system integration on a 12 kg tensegrity robot confirms the sensor's ability to reliably identify ground contacts. The system substantially improves the practicality of tactile feedback for tensegrity robots, offering a low-cost, reproducible, and physically interpretable pathway toward contact-aware proprioception and state estimation. Open source files are available at \href{https://github.com/Jonathan-Twz/tensegrity-gelfoot}{github.com/Jonathan-Twz/tensegrity-gelfoot}

</details>


### [80] [IMBench: A Benchmark for Intuitive Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15641)
*Anurag Maurya,Sukhvansh Jain,Prajwal Avhad,Gautham Balachandran,Ziyi Zhou,Atharva Kshirsagar,Satyam Singh,Bowen Li. Rishabh Mukund,Ritul Singh,Jatin Vira,Suvonil Chatterjee,Devesh K. Jha*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: IMBENCH是一个评估直觉操作能力的基准，包含35个任务和14K过滤轨迹。实验发现，视觉语言模型具备部分物理推理能力但无法生成可执行计划，而视觉-语言-动作模型难以满足任务约束和泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准将物理推理与执行分离评估，未能捕捉人类整合推理与运动控制解决复杂操作任务的能力。

Method: 设计了35个任务、14K过滤轨迹和可扩展场景生成工具，评估模型在感知、物理推理、动作生成和迭代执行四方面的综合能力，任务涵盖接触丰富操作、工具使用和多阶段依赖。

Result: 视觉语言模型有部分物理推理能力但无法生成可执行计划；视觉-语言-动作模型难以满足任务约束且泛化能力不足。

Conclusion: 直觉操作是当前基础模型和通用机器人策略中缺失的能力维度，IMBENCH为评估和实现更集成、自适应的物理智能提供了初步框架。

Abstract: Humans combine reasoning and motor control to solve complex manipulation tasks under diverse constraints. They build an understanding of the physical world that helps them convert reasoning into actions and quickly adapt to new scenes, tasks, and rules. We refer to this capability as intuitive manipulation. Existing benchmarks fail to capture this integration: they evaluate physical reasoning in isolation from execution, or measure policy performance without requiring explicit reasoning. We introduce IMBENCH, a benchmark designed to evaluate intuitive manipulation as an integrated capability spanning perception, physical reasoning, action generation, and iterative execution. Our tasks require models to infer task-relevant physical structure and generate feasible action sequences under explicit constraints, including contact-rich manipulation, tool use, and multi-stage dependencies. We introduce a benchmark of 35 tasks, 14K filtered trajectories, and scalable tools for generating diverse scenarios. Experiments reveal a consistent gap: vision language models show partial physical reasoning ability but fail to produce executable plans, while state-of-the-art vision-language-action models struggle to satisfy task constraints and generalize across scenarios. These results identify intuitive manipulation as a missing axis in current foundation models and generalist robot policies, and position IMBENCH as a step toward evaluating and enabling more integrated, adaptive physical intelligence.

</details>


### [81] [Difference-Based Relational Learning for Zero-Shot Object-Goal Visual Navigation With Direct Sim-to-Real Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15642)
*Guolei Qi,Feitian Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出T-DRN网络，通过结合孪生差异特征提取器和双帧时间缓冲器，实现了从仿真到真实场景的零样本目标导航，有效克服了外观变化和窄视场角限制，在AI2-THOR仿真和实体机器人上均验证了优越性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端深度强化学习在零样本目标视觉导航中面临仿真到真实的差距问题，特别是目标外观变化和相机视场角受限两大挑战。

Method: 提出T-DRN网络，包含两个核心组件：(1) 孪生差异特征提取器，计算目标与观测对象之间的关系差异，生成域无关的表征；(2) 双帧时间缓冲器，在窄视场角下保持短期目标连续性。

Result: 在AI2-THOR仿真环境中，T-DRN在成功率上显著优于强基线方法；在物理轮式机器人上，该网络在真实传感与执行约束下表现出鲁棒性能。

Conclusion: T-DRN验证了从仿真到真实环境直接迁移的可行性，为零样本目标视觉导航中的sim-to-real问题提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: End-to-end deep reinforcement learning (DRL) for zero-shot object-goal visual navigation remains challenged by the sim-to-real gap, particularly variations in object appearance and restricted camera field-of-view (FoV). This letter proposes a Temporal Difference-Relational Network (T-DRN) for robust zero-shot sim-to-real transfer. T-DRN combines a Siamese difference-based feature extractor, which computes relational difference between the target and observed objects to produce domain-independent representations, with a dual-frame temporal buffer that preserves short-term object continuity under narrow FoV. Extensive experiments in AI2-THOR demonstrate that T-DRN improves zero-shot generalization in terms of success rates over strong baselines. Furthermore, T-DRN is systematically validated on a physical wheeled robot, demonstrating robust performance under real sensing and actuation constraints and supporting the feasibility of direct sim-to-real transfer.

</details>


### [82] [Learning a System-Level Surrogate for Hydraulic Excavators: A Simulation-to-Real LSTM Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15656)
*Shuai Wang,Shen Wang,Qiang Wang,Muguo Du,Donghai Shi,Chenyu Wang,Xiaofeng Tao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于LSTM网络的仿真到现实（Sim-to-Real）框架，通过学习系统级数字代理模型来模拟液压挖掘机的闭环行为。该方法将挖掘机视为输入-输出算子，先在MuJoCo仿真环境验证，再迁移到真实挖掘机，并引入基于自适应卡尔曼滤波的一致性感知状态估计方法处理真实数据中的测量不一致问题。实验表明该代理模型在角速度和长时程轨迹复现上具有高保真度，可为挖掘自动化算法开发提供可扩展的替代方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主液压挖掘机开发受限于物理机器获取困难以及真实世界实验成本高昂，迫切需要一种能够高效、低成本进行算法开发和验证的方法。

Method: 1) 将挖掘机视为输入-输出算子，使用LSTM网络学习系统级数字代理模型，而非建模内部动力学；2) 在MuJoCo仿真环境中进行初步验证；3) 引入基于自适应卡尔曼滤波的一致性感知状态估计方法，解决真实世界数据中测量不一致问题；4) 将模型从仿真迁移到真实挖掘机。

Result: 所学习的代理模型在闭环自回归评估下，能够在角速度和长时程轨迹复现方面达到高保真度，验证了该模型可作为仿真和物理系统的直接替代方案。

Conclusion: 该Sim-to-Real框架能够为挖掘自动化算法的开发提供可扩展且高效的方案，减少对物理机器和真实实验的依赖。

Abstract: Developing autonomous hydraulic excavators is constrained by limited access to physical machines and the high cost of real-world experimentation. This paper proposes a simulation-to-real framework for learning a system-level digital surrogate using Long Short-Term Memory (LSTM) networks. Instead of modeling internal dynamics, the excavator is treated as an input-output operator, and the surrogate is trained to reproduce its closed-loop behavior under identical control inputs. The approach is first validated in a MuJoCo simulation environment and then transferred to a real excavator. To address measurement inconsistencies in real-world data, a consistency-aware state estimation method based on adaptive Kalman filtering is introduced. Experimental results demonstrate that the learned surrogate achieves high fidelity in both angular velocity and long-horizon trajectory reproduction under closed-loop autoregressive evaluation. These results confirm that the proposed model can serve as a drop-in surrogate for both simulation and physical systems, enabling scalable and efficient development of excavation automation algorithms.

</details>


### [83] [Continuously Stable Structure through Plastic Deformation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15659)
*Junlong Xiao,Yaoqiang Pan,Xuan Zhang,Michael Yu Wang,Chao Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种通过塑性变形实现连续稳定结构（CSSPD）的软体夹爪，无需能量输入即可维持稳定构型，结合仿生爪垫与kirigami金属层优化，实现了高达16N的被动保持力（媲美0.3MPa气动驱动），并能承受400 m/s²脉冲加速度，可长期无动力栖息于树枝上，适用于移动机器人应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软体机器人因其柔顺性和适应性被广泛用于交互任务，但其稳定性不足，尤其在动态环境中软体夹爪容易因加速度或外部扰动导致抓取失败。本文旨在通过塑性变形机制解决软体夹爪的静动态稳定性问题。

Method: 提出CSSPD结构，利用塑性变形使夹爪无需能量输入即可保持连续构型；引入仿生爪垫增强稳定性并支持基于感知的快速抓取；建立数学模型并优化金属层的kirigami结构。

Result: 夹爪无需能量输入可提供高达16N的被动保持力（性能媲美0.3MPa气动驱动）；结合气动驱动可在400 m/s²脉冲加速度下保持稳定；可无动力长时间栖息于树枝上。

Conclusion: CSSPD结构有效解决了软体夹爪的稳定性难题，实现了无需持续能量输入的稳定抓取与动态环境适应能力，为移动机器人应用提供了新方案。

Abstract: Soft robots have seen widespread adoption in interactive tasks due to their inherent compliance and adaptability. However, these advantages often come at the cost of stability, posing challenges in a dynamic environment. This limitation is especially critical in soft grippers, where instability under acceleration or external disturbances can result in grasp failure. In this study, we present a continuously stable structure through plastic deformation (CSSPD), integrated into a soft gripper. By leveraging the mechanism of plastic deformation, the gripper maintains continuous configurations without energy input, while the added stiffness ensures both static and dynamic stability. We introduce a bioinspired paw pad that significantly enhances stability and enables sensing-based rapid object grasping. Then we develop the mathematical model and optimize the kirigami structure of the metal layer. Experimental results show that the gripper can sustain a passive holding force of up to 16 N without energy input, achieving performance comparable to pneumatic actuation at 0.3 MPa. When combined with pneumatic actuation, it remains stable under pulsed accelerations of up to 400 m/s^2. It can also passively perch on tree branches for extended periods without power, demonstrating promise for mobile robotic applications.

</details>


### [84] [A Generative Partially Specified Finite State Machine Approach to Complex Behaviour Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15674)
*Kalana Ratnayake,Michael Pritchard,David Hinwood,Maleen Jayasuriya,Damith Herath*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种生成式部分指定有限状态机（GPSFSM）神经符号架构，首次将FSM用于机器人行为规划的生成式方法，通过Fabric引擎、异步事件系统和PromptTools组件，在导航任务中实现了比当前最先进的BTGenBot系统更高的规划成功率，特别是在零样本场景中表现优异，同时保持较低延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然大语言模型已成功与行为树结合用于动态重规划，但有限状态机因其广泛采用和计算效率高，在生成式方法中尚未被探索。现有生成系统存在临时函数表示的限制，需要一种结合FSM符号结构与语义结构的生成式行为规划框架。

Method: 提出GPSFSM神经符号架构，包含三个核心组件：1) Fabric——FSM引擎，用于解析、验证和执行包含顺序、恢复、并行任意和并行全部四种控制结构的行为规划；2) 在ROS2的Capabilities2包中扩展异步事件系统，实现行为链式调用和运行时参数注入；3) PromptTools——统一ROS2接口，支持本地和云端LLM的提示缓冲和动态异步组合。

Result: 在导航任务实验中，GPSFSM方法在规划生成成功率上持续优于当前最先进的BTGenBot系统，尤其在行为树难以处理的零样本场景中表现突出，同时保持与前沿LLM相当或更低的规划延迟。系统还能生成复杂行为。

Conclusion: 本文展示了生成式FSM规划在机器人系统中的实用性和可复现性，通过开源ROS2栈为机器人行为规划提供了新的范式。GPSFSM结合了FSM的计算效率和LLM的灵活推理能力，特别适合需要零样本泛化的动态环境。

Abstract: Autonomous robots operating in dynamic environments require behaviour planning systems that combine reactivity, interpretability, and adaptability. While Large Language Models have been successfully integrated with Behaviour Trees for dynamic replanning, Finite State Machines, despite their widespread adoption and computational efficiency, remain unexplored for generative approaches. We propose a Generative Partially Specified Finite State Machine (GPSFSM) neurosymbolic architecture that utilises the symbolic and semantic structure of FSMs, including states and event-triggered transitions, to implement Behaviour Planning. This paper introduces the first GPSFSM framework for robotics, featuring Fabric, an FSM engine that parses, validates, and executes behaviour plans that contain Sequential, Recovery, Parallel-Any, and Parallel-All control structures. We extend the Capabilities2 package in ROS2 with an asynchronous event system for behaviour chaining and runtime parameter injection for configurable execution, addressing the ad-hoc function representations that limit current generative systems. PromptTools provides a unified ROS 2 interface to local and cloud LLMs, with prompt buffering, enabling dynamic asynchronous composition of task and context information. Together, these components enable standardised semantic capability descriptions for robot-agnostic development. Experimental evaluation on navigation tasks demonstrates that our GPSFSM approach achieves consistently higher plan-generation success rates than the state-of-the-art BTGenBot system, particularly excelling in zero-shot scenarios where BTs typically struggle, while maintaining comparable or lower planning latency to frontier LLMs. We also demonstrate that our system can generate complex behaviours. We release an open-source ROS2 stack that makes generative FSM planning practical and reproducible for robotic systems.

</details>


### [85] [RAVEN: Reinforcement-Adaptive Visibility-Graph Planning for Robust Humanoid Navigation with Collision-Free MPC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15701)
*Ruochen Hou,Shiqi Wang,Beom Jun Kim,Hanzhang Fang,Mehak Singal,Dennis W. Hong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RAVEN提出了一种分层强化学习-模型预测控制框架，通过RL自适应调整可见性图规划器的障碍物膨胀等几何参数，再结合MPC进行约束跟踪，实现了对人形机器人在动态环境中鲁棒且可解释的导航，优于手动调参基线和纯RL策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典可见性图规划器+MPC方法依赖手动调参和精确系统建模，在存在控制延迟、状态估计噪声和 locomotion 不确定性的真实机器人系统中，几何最优路径仍会导致过冲和约束违反。

Method: 采用分层RL-MPC框架：上层RL通过学习调整可见性图规划器的障碍物膨胀参数和图形构造参数，重塑自由空间几何结构以改变全局路径拓扑；下层碰撞避免MPC层跟踪规划轨迹，显式强制执行速度边界和避障约束。在包含延迟和观测噪声的仿真环境中训练。

Result: 相比手动调参的可见性图-MPC基线和纯RL导航策略，RAVEN在障碍物附近过冲更小、狭窄通道鲁棒性更强、在延迟和噪声下导航更可靠。

Conclusion: 强化自适应图构造结合约束MPC为鲁棒人形导航提供了一种有效且可解释的替代方案，优于端到端学习方法。

Abstract: Humanoid navigation in dynamic environments requires long-horizon planning while respecting short-horizon dynamic and safety constraints. Classical visibility-graph planners combined with model predictive control (MPC) can efficiently generate collision-free trajectories, but their performance depends on manually tuned parameters and accurate system modeling. In real robotic systems, control delays, state-estimation noise, and locomotion uncertainties can cause overshoot and constraint violations even when the nominal path is geometrically optimal. We propose RAVEN, a hierarchical reinforcement learning (RL)-MPC framework for robust humanoid navigation. Unlike prior approaches that use learning to tune cost weights or replace planning entirely, RAVEN employs RL to adapt the geometric construction of a visibility-graph planner by modifying obstacle inflation and related graph parameters. By directly reshaping the free-space geometry, the learned planner alters the topology of the global path to compensate for delay and tracking imperfections. A collision-free MPC layer then tracks the planned trajectory while explicitly enforcing velocity bounds and obstacle-avoidance constraints. By training under realistic delays and observation noise, RAVEN learns planning adaptations that improve robustness while retaining explicit long-horizon geometric planning and constrained optimization, in contrast to end-to-end learning approaches. We evaluate RAVEN against a manually tuned visibility-graph MPC baseline and a pure RL navigation policy. Results demonstrate reduced overshoot near obstacles, improved robustness in narrow passages, and more reliable navigation under delay and noise. These findings indicate that reinforcement-adaptive graph construction combined with constrained MPC provides an effective and interpretable alternative to end-to-end learning for robust humanoid navigation.

</details>


### [86] [Implicit Virtual Leader: Decentralized Vision-Only Relative Pose Estimation for Multi-Robot Formations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15708)
*Shiyuan Yang,Zelin Wang,Zhijia Tao,Yilin Wang,Zhengyu Hou,Xiaosong Kong,Borong Zhang,Yip Fun Yeung,Yuankai Luo,Sharon Lee,Qingbiao Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于图神经网络的全分散视觉相对位姿估计框架，通过隐式虚拟领导者（IVL）概念，仅依赖单目图像和机器人间通信实现编队控制，避免了单点故障、误差传播和GPS依赖问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统领导者-跟随者编队控制存在单点故障、误差传播问题，且依赖绝对定位传感器，在GPS拒止环境中效果不佳。本文旨在通过全分散的视觉相对位姿估计框架解决这些局限性。

Method: 利用图神经网络（GNN）学习隐式虚拟领导者（IVL）作为非物理编队参考系；通过单目图像和机器人间通信实现全分散位姿估计；附加异方差高斯负对数似然（GNLL）头建模偶然不确定性，MC Dropout建模认知不确定性。

Result: 在仿真和真实世界测试集上进行了系统比较，实现了有竞争力的位姿估计精度，并自然地泛化到异构机器人平台和不同编队规模。

Conclusion: 所提框架有效克服了传统方法的缺陷，在GPS拒止环境中具有实用价值，且具有良好的泛化能力。

Abstract: Classical leader-follower formation control suffers from single points of failure and error propagation, and relies on absolute localization sensors that are ill-suited for GPS-denied environments. We address these limitations by introducing a fully decentralized, vision-only relative pose estimation framework based on Graph Neural Networks (GNNs). The key idea is the implicit virtual leader (IVL): a non-physical formation reference frame that is not tied to any individual robot but is implicitly learned within the GNN using only monocular images and inter-robot communication. We attach a heteroscedastic GNLL head for aleatoric uncertainty and MC~Dropout for epistemic uncertainty, and conduct a systematic comparison across simulation and real-world test sets. Our framework achieves competitive pose estimation accuracy and generalizes naturally to heterogeneous robot platforms and varying formation sizes.

</details>


### [87] [AC-VLA: Robust Out-of-Distribution Action Execution via Compositional Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15714)
*Xiaojiang Peng,Kai Peng,Jie Lu,Zheng Lian,Zitong YU,Xiaobo Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AC-VLA框架通过指令分解与轨迹对齐模块实现组合学习，结合状态条件非对称掩码策略抑制腕部视图过拟合，在无需修改架构的情况下，使VLA模型在组合OOD任务上提升约28%绝对性能，同时保持近完美的分布内表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型在端到端机器人操作中表现优异，但面对熟悉子任务以未见配置重新组合时，存在分布外泛化困难。论文识别出两个相互强化的失败模式：轨迹过拟合（模型过度拟合整体轨迹模式而非组合性子技能语义）和感知捷径（动作标记过度依赖腕部视角纹理而牺牲全局空间定位）。

Method: 提出AC-VLA框架，包含两个核心组件：(i) 组合学习模块，利用LLM驱动的指令分解器和本体感受轨迹对齐器生成密集子任务监督，通过完整演示与分解数据的混合训练赋予模型组合泛化能力；(ii) 状态条件非对称掩码策略，在夹爪闭合阶段抑制腕部视图输入，强制模型依赖全局语义定位。所有组件无需修改架构，可直接集成到任何VLA骨干网络中。

Result: 在π_{0.5}上实例化，基于LIBERO和LIBERO-OOD基准评估，AC-VLA在组合OOD任务上实现约28%的绝对性能提升，同时保持近完美的分布内性能。

Conclusion: AC-VLA通过组合学习与状态条件掩码策略，有效解决了VLA模型在组合泛化场景下的轨迹过拟合与感知捷径问题，为无架构改动的即插即用解决方案提供了实证支持。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models excel at end-to-end robotic manipulation but struggle with out-of-distribution (OOD) generalization when familiar sub-tasks are recombined in unseen configurations. We identify two mutually reinforcing failure modes: \emph{trajectory overfitting}, where models overfit to holistic trajectory patterns rather than compositional sub-skill semantics; and \emph{perceptual shortcut}, where action tokens over-rely on wrist-view textures at the expense of global spatial grounding. To address both, we introduce \textbf{AC-VLA}, a plug-and-play Action Compositional learning framework comprising two architecture-agnostic components: \textbf{(i)} a compositional learning module that uses an LLM-driven instruction decomposer and a proprioceptive trajectory aligner to generate dense sub-task supervision, followed by mixed training on complete demonstrations and decomposed data to endow the model with compositional generalization; and \textbf{(ii)} a state-conditioned asymmetric masking strategy that suppresses wrist-view inputs during closed-gripper phases, enforcing global semantic grounding. All components are architectural modification-free and directly integrable into any VLA backbone. Instantiated on $π_{0.5}$ and evaluated on LIBERO and LIBERO-OOD benchmarks, AC-VLA achieves a ~28% absolute improvement on compositional OOD tasks while maintaining near-perfect in-distribution performance.

</details>


### [88] [A Task-Space Receding Horizon Controller for Fast Collision Avoidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15733)
*Mattia Penzotti,Marco Controzzi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种任务空间滚动时域控制器，通过短时接触一致性滚动生成终端运动学参考，在避免全轨迹优化的同时实现实时避障，并在40自由度多链系统和6自由度平台上验证了其性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全模型预测控制在线计算成本随预测时域、模型保真度和几何约束数量快速增长，而无时域反应式方法在动态杂乱环境中缺乏远见，需在计算效率和前瞻性之间取得平衡。

Method: 采用任务空间滚动时域控制，通过短时接触一致性滚动（基于膨胀凸几何的迭代动力学求解器）生成终端运动学参考，满足内部非穿透约束；仅计算朝向该参考的平滑最小加速度过渡的第一个输入。基准则为闭环逆运动学调节律。

Result: 在40自由度多链系统仿真中验证了中等时域可平衡预见性、响应性和计算成本；6自由度平台硬件实验展示了一致的仿真到现实行为，在动态杂乱环境中比动态优化织物和MPC基线获得更高成功率，同时保持实时求解时间。

Conclusion: 该方法在避免全约束轨迹优化的前提下实现了前瞻性避障，具有局部指数收敛保证，且对惯性参数估计误差具有鲁棒性，适合实时执行。

Abstract: Real-time collision avoidance for robotic manipulators requires fast reactions to unexpected obstacle motion and lookahead to avoid becoming trapped by near-future constraints. Full model predictive control can provide this foresight, but its online cost may grow quickly with horizon length, model fidelity, and the number of active geometric constraints. Conversely, horizon-free reactive methods are computationally efficient but can be short-sighted in dynamic clutter. We present a task-space receding-horizon controller that uses a short contact-consistent rollout to generate a terminal kinematic reference satisfying internal non-penetration constraints, then computes only the first input of a smooth minimum-acceleration transition toward that reference. Starting from a closed-loop inverse-kinematics regulation law, the rollout is performed with an iterative dynamics solver operating on inflated convex robot and obstacle geometries, so that robot-obstacle contacts, dynamic obstacle motion, and self-collisions can shape the terminal reference without requiring full constrained trajectory optimization. We analyze the contact-inactive closed loop and show local exponential task-space regulation under standard regularity assumptions. For contacts activated inside the rollout, we characterize the corresponding discrete updates and bound the effect of moving obstacles on regular operating sets. Simulations on a 40-DOF multi-chain system show that intermediate horizons balance anticipation, responsiveness, and computational cost. Hardware experiments on a 6-DOF platform demonstrate consistent sim-to-real behavior without accurate inertial parameter estimation, and comparisons against dynamic optimization fabrics and model predictive control (MPC) baselines show improved success rates in dynamic clutter while preserving solve times compatible with real-time execution in the tested regimes.

</details>


### [89] [Towards Artificial Nerves: Biomimetic Optical-Fiber Tactile Sensing for Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15746)
*Laura E. Butcher,Chris J. Ford,Nathan F. Lepora,Efi Psomopoulou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: OptiTac是一种仿生触觉传感器，通过将机械触针与光纤（人工神经）配对，模仿人类皮肤的机械感受器-神经结构，将触觉信息从感知表面传输出去，同时保持高空间分辨率，并用简单解析方法（而非深度学习）推断接触位置、大小和形状，为可解释、可扩展的分布式触觉传感提供了新路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人系统需要接近人类皮肤适应性和分辨率的触觉传感能力，以实现灵巧操作和安全交互；现有触觉传感器在空间分辨率与信息传输之间难以兼顾，且缺乏可解释性。

Method: 采用仿生设计，将每个机械触针与一根光纤（人工神经）配对，构成类似机械感受器到神经的架构；将触觉信号视为图像，使用简单的解析方法（而非深度学习）推断接触位置、大小和形状。

Result: 实现了将触觉信息从传感表面传输出去的同时保留高空间分辨率；通过简单的解析方法即可准确推断接触位置、大小和形状，避免了对不透明深度学习模型的依赖。

Conclusion: OptiTac展示了生物学进化原理可指导机器人人工神经系统的开发，为下一代机器人平台实现类人触觉感知提供了可行的途径，并为未来分布式触觉系统建立了可解释、可扩展的人工神经传感框架。

Abstract: Robotic systems increasingly demand tactile sensing that approaches the adaptability and resolution of human skin to enable dexterous manipulation and safe interaction. OptiTac is a biomimetic tactile sensor that emulates the mechanoreceptor-to-nerve architecture of human touch by pairing each mechanical pin on a soft skin with an optical fiber acting as an artificial nerve. This design demonstrates an architectural principle for routing tactile information away from the sensing surface while preserving high spatial resolution, establishing a practical route toward distributed tactile sensing in future robotic systems. By treating tactile signals as images, simple analytical methods, rather than opaque deep-learning models, are used to infer contact location, size, and shape, providing interpretable and scalable tactile intelligence. This work demonstrates how evolutionary principles from biology can guide the development of artificial nerve systems for robots, offering a pathway toward human-like tactile perception in next-generation robotic platforms. More broadly, OptiTac establishes an artificial nerve-inspired sensing framework for interpretable robotic touch and a scalable route toward future distributed tactile systems.

</details>


### [90] [SkillNav: Score-Level Skill Intervention for Zero-Shot Object Goal Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15758)
*Ruijie Sang,Yiqun Duan,Pinhan Fu,Ruilin Wang,Wei Sui,Xianda Guo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SkillNav提出了一种可扩展的行为技能框架，通过在价值地图上写入行为记忆（零token成本），以三层技能协作机制解决VLM导航代理中单帧推理导致的死胡同、房间内循环等问题，无需训练即实现了MP3D、HM3D等多个导航基准上的新SOTA SPL和成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM导航代理依赖单帧推理，缺乏跨步骤行为感知能力，导致死胡同停滞、室内循环、接近目标时绕路等重复性失败。基于prompt的补救方案会大幅增加token预算，且难以有效编码角度、地图单元、视角坐标等空间信号。

Method: SkillNav是一个可扩展的行为技能框架，核心创新包括：(1) 将VLM导航器已有的好奇心价值地图作为可写入的底层媒介，技能以零token成本在其上刻写行为记忆；(2) 技能分为三个层级：软缩放（比例重加权）、下界提升（区域级保证）、硬覆盖（阈值触发强制动作），按固定组合顺序协作形成可预测的优先级声明；(3) 最小化prompt通道提供类别级语义提示，与地图上的空间记忆形成双表征设计。

Result: 无需训练，在MP3D上SPL达25.5，HM3D v0.1上SPL达39.3、SR达69.7，HM3D v0.2上SPL达43.2、SR达75.9，SPL较最强先前方法提升最高达6.0绝对值。

Conclusion: SkillNav通过将能力改进转化为技能注册（新行为可即插即用，无需重训练VLM或干扰已有技能），开辟了持续精炼的路径，在多个基准上建立了新的SOTA。

Abstract: Vision-Language Model (VLM) agents have advanced zero-shot object-goal navigation, yet single-frame reasoning leaves them without the cross-step behavioral awareness an embodied navigator requires, producing recurring failures such as dead-end stalls, in-room loops, and circuitous approaches to detected targets. Prompt-based remedies inflate token budgets across multi-submodule episodes and still struggle to encode inherently spatial signals such as angles, map cells, and viewpoint coordinates. In this paper, we propose SkillNav, an extensible behavioral skill framework for VLM-based navigation that treats the curiosity value map already maintained by modern VLM navigators as a writable substrate on which composable skills inscribe behavioral memory at zero token cost. Skills are stratified into three tiers by their level of behavioral authority, namely soft scaling for proportional reweighting, lower-bound boost for region-level guarantees, and hard override for threshold-triggered forced actions, and cooperate across tiers under a fixed composition order that establishes a predictable, declared priority among skills. This design turns capability improvement into skill registration: new behaviors plug in without retraining the VLM or disturbing existing skills, opening a path for continual refinement. A minimal prompt channel complements the score-level skills with category-level semantic hints, yielding a dual-representation design in which spatial memory lives on the map and semantic memory in short prompts. Training-free, SkillNav establishes new state-of-the-art SPL across MP3D (25.5), HM3D v0.1 (39.3), and HM3D v0.2 (43.2), improving SPL by up to 6.0 absolute over the strongest prior method, and achieves the highest Success Rate on HM3D v0.1 (69.7) and v0.2 (75.9).

</details>


### [91] [Deployment-Ready UWB Localization for Industrial Ground Robots with Automatic Anchor Calibration and Terrain-Aware Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15807)
*Alexander Raab,Giulio Delama,Roland Jung,Stephan Weiss*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向工业自主移动机器人的UWB定位全流程方案，包含自动锚点标定和通用多传感器融合估计器，通过偏差感知扩展卡尔曼滤波实现高精度定位，在仓储场景和叉车数据集上验证了效果，显著降低了人工标定成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: UWB测距虽在工业AMR定位中展现出潜力，但实际部署面临两大挑战：静态锚点标定耗时且易出错，以及UWB与现有板载传感器的融合需要精心设计才能保证鲁棒一致的位姿估计。本文旨在解决这些问题。

Method: 提出了端到端流水线：标定阶段估计锚点位置和距离偏差；定位阶段将UWB与本体感知融合到偏差感知扩展卡尔曼滤波中，针对平面运动车辆进行定制，无需大量参数调优即可提升一致性。

Result: 在商业化物流AMR的仓储环境中实现了室内及室内外过渡的准确定位，相比先前估计器公式一致性提升；在独立叉车数据集上的评估表明可迁移至其他平台；在有限视距和稀疏锚点覆盖下依然有效。

Conclusion: UWB定位可以在大幅减少人力投入的前提下部署，同时保持工业AMR所需的精度。仓储数据集已公开。

Abstract: Ultra-Wideband (UWB) ranging has become a viable option for industrial Autonomous Mobile Robot (AMR) localization due to improved accuracy and low cost. However, real-world deployments remain limited by two recurring challenges: calibrating static anchors can be time-consuming and error-prone, and integrating UWB with existing onboard sensors requires careful design to ensure robust and consistent pose estimation. Addressing these challenges, this paper presents an end-to-end pipeline that combines automatic anchor calibration with a generic multi-sensor estimator tailored to surface-bound vehicle motion. It targets existing AMR stacks in scenarios where robot pose priors are available for initialization. The calibration stage estimates anchor positions and range biases, while the localization stage fuses UWB with proprioceptive sensing in a bias-aware Extended Kalman Filter to improve consistency without extensive parameter tuning. Experiments on a commercial logistics AMR in a warehouse setting demonstrate accurate positioning indoors and across outdoor transitions, with improved consistency compared to an earlier estimator formulation. Evaluation on an independent forklift dataset further indicates transferability to other platforms. The method remains effective in test cases with limited line-of-sight and sparse anchor coverage. These results show that UWB localization can be deployed with substantially reduced manual effort while preserving the accuracy required for industrial AMRs. The collected warehouse dataset is made publicly available.

</details>


### [92] [Beyond Frontiers: Scene-Anomaly Guided Autonomous Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15828)
*Akash Kumbar,Abhinav Raundhal,Madhava Krishna*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出SCAGE框架，将自主3D环境探索重新定义为几何异常最小化问题，通过检测场景中与预期室内结构不符的异常区域来引导机器人探索，以同时实现高覆盖率和高质量3D重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于覆盖率最大化的几何启发式探索方法忽略底层结构上下文，导致轨迹效率低下且最终3D重建质量受限。本文旨在弥合空间覆盖与重建质量之间的鸿沟。

Method: 提出SCAGE框架：1）赋予机器人对标准室内结构的先验理解；2）机器人导航时持续将实时3D观测与学习到的期望进行比较；3）将不符合先验的区域（如破碎的墙壁、不完整的桌子）标记为场景异常；4）以这些几何不一致性作为引导信号，驱动机器人从最佳视角调查和解决这些结构异常。

Result: 在各类场景中实现约90%的体素覆盖率，且3D重建质量显著优于现有基线方法。

Conclusion: 通过主动瞄准重建不佳的区域而非空白空间，SCAGE有效将空间发现与高保真建图无缝耦合，证明了基于场景异常引导的探索范式在自主3D探索中的优越性。

Abstract: Autonomous exploration of unknown 3D environments is traditionally driven by coverage-maximizing geometric heuristics. However, these methods typically determine exploration targets without considering the underlying structural context. This leads to inefficient trajectories often limiting the fidelity of the final 3D reconstruction. To bridge the gap between spatial coverage and reconstruction quality, we introduce a novel paradigm: reframing exploration as a geometric anomaly minimization problem. We present SCAGE: SCene Anomaly Guided Exploration, a novel autonomous exploration framework that operates directly on unstructured 3D point clouds. Instead of blindly chasing volumetric boundaries, we equip the robot with a foundational understanding of standard indoor architecture. As the robot navigates, it continuously evaluates its live 3D observations against these learned expectations. When the incoming geometry contradicts the learned priors of a typical indoor environment, such as a fragmented wall or a partial table, the system flags these regions as scene anomalies. These geometric inconsistencies act as a guiding signal, naturally drawing the robot to investigate and resolve these structural anomalies from optimal vantage points. By actively targeting poorly reconstructed regions rather than just empty space, our approach seamlessly couples spatial discovery with high-fidelity mapping. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that SCAGE achieves superior volumetric coverage (~90% in all scenes) and higher 3D reconstruction quality compared to state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>


### [93] [Minimum Time Dubins Airplane Paths with Asymmetric Climb Rates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15863)
*Jaeyoung Lim,Giuseppe Loianno*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出非对称Dubins航路，通过考虑爬升和下降时不同的爬升率约束，打破了传统对称爬升率限制，使固定翼飞行器能够充分利用自身性能，在随机生成状态间最短时间减少71%，中位求解速度提升2.8倍，并通过真实飞行验证了实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Dubins航路假设爬升率对称约束，限制了固定翼飞行器实际性能的发挥，导致路径次优且性能保守。本文旨在通过引入非对称爬升率来充分利用飞行器的全部机动能力。

Method: 提出了非对称Dubins航路模型，允许爬升和下降阶段具有不同的爬升率约束；重新推导了时间最优性条件，证明了非对称飞行路径角约束下最优性仍可保持；并将该模型集成到基于采样的规划框架中。

Result: 随机生成状态间最短时间减少71%；中位求解速度提升2.8倍；通过真实飞行验证了方法的实用性。

Conclusion: 非对称爬升率约束能更充分利用飞行器性能，生成更优路径，且在实际任务中可行有效。

Abstract: Dubins airplane paths approximate the limited maneuverability of fixed-wing vehicles with minimum curvature and climb rate constraints. However, the symmetric climb rate constraints result in sub-optimal paths and conservative vehicle performance. In this work, we propose asymmetric Dubins airplane paths, which consider asymmetric climb rates for climbing and descending. We revisit the time optimality conditions and show that the asymmetric flight path angle constraints preserve optimality. We show that by considering asymmetric climb rates, we can take advantage of full performance of the vehicle, reducing the minimum time by 71% for connecting randomly generated states. We also demonstrate that the added climb rate results in 2.8 times faster to find the median solution time when integrated into a sampling-based planning task on rugged terrain, due to the added feasibility. We further demonstrate the practicality of the approach with a real-world flight.

</details>


### [94] [Dynamics-Aware Meta-Imitation for Generalization to Unseen Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15880)
*Zhenduo Shang,Xiyao Liu,Bohan Li,Xudong Wang,Teng Ren,Lianqing Liu,Zhi Han*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出DAMI框架，通过元学习构建共享技能空间，结合VMT模块捕捉时空动态、U2T块融合非结构化多模态观测、TCFM机制调制3D特征，从完整参考演示中学习内在任务逻辑，在仿真和真实场景中均优于现有方法，在已见任务直接推理和未见任务少样本微调上表现更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模仿学习方法主要聚焦于域内任务，难以泛化到未见过的新任务，存在数据稀缺和环境泛化两大瓶颈。

Method: 提出Dynamics-Aware Meta-Imitation (DAMI)框架，包含三个核心模块：Visual-Motor Trajectory (VMT)模块用于捕捉任务潜在空间中的复杂时空动态；Unpaired Unified Task (U2T)块用于融合非结构化多模态观测；Task-Conditioned Feature Modulation (TCFM)机制用于调制低层3D特征。通过元学习构建共享技能空间，从随机完整参考演示中学习内在任务逻辑。

Result: 在仿真和真实场景实验中，DAMI在已见任务的直接推断和未见过任务的少样本微调适应上均优于当前最先进基线方法。

Conclusion: 通过学习任务内在动态逻辑而非记忆静态线索，DAMI有效弥合了模仿学习的泛化差距，实现了对未见任务的有效泛化。

Abstract: Imitation Learning aims to learn skills from extensive observations and demonstrations for robots, so it suffers from data scarcity and environment generalization. The existing methods predominantly focus on imitation from in-domain tasks and consequently struggle with generalization to unseen tasks. To bridge this generalization gap, we propose the \textbf{D}ynamics-\textbf{A}ware \textbf{M}eta-\textbf{I}mitation (DAMI) framework. By integrating meta-learning to construct a shared skill space, DAMI equips agents for rapid adaptation to novel tasks. We introduce the Visual-Motor Trajectory (VMT) module to capture complex spatio-temporal dynamics within the task latent space. Furthermore, we propose the Unpaired Unified Task (U2T) block to fuse unstructured multimodal observations. To coordinate these representations, we integrate a Task-Conditioned Feature Modulation (TCFM) mechanism customized for modulating low-level 3D features. By capturing intrinsic dynamics from a random complete reference demonstration, our framework learns the underlying task logic rather than memorizing static cues, ensuring effective generalization. Extensive experiments in both simulation and real-world settings demonstrate that our approach outperforms state-of-the-art baselines regarding direct inference on seen tasks and adaptation to unseen tasks via few-shot fine-tuning.

</details>


### [95] [Learning Reach-Avoid Task with Reinforcement Learning: Vectorized Simulation and Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15935)
*Jonas Weihing,Shahram Eivazi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个无需简化的、面向真实复杂场景的机械臂避障任务综合基准，利用MuJoCo MJX和Brax库实现并行化，在UR5e和Franka机器人上分别达到了96.1%/98.8%的到达任务成功率和86.8%/95.2%的静态避障成功率，并发现DRL在简化场景中表现完美但在真实场景中性能大幅下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DRL在机械臂避障任务上的研究多局限于简化的桌面场景，缺乏能真实反映现实复杂性的基准环境，无法评估DRL在真实场景下的实际能力。

Method: 利用MuJoCo MJX物理引擎和Brax库实现仿真环境与DRL算法的并行化，构建了多样化、无简化的机械臂避障任务基准，在UR5e和Franka Emika Robot上进行了评估。

Result: 到达任务成功率：UR5e 96.1%、Franka 98.8%；静态避障任务成功率：UR5e 86.8%、Franka 95.2%。DRL在简化场景中表现完美，但在真实场景中性能显著下降。

Conclusion: 尽管DRL在简化设置中能完美解决到达任务，但在真实复杂场景中性能崩溃，表明仍需进一步研究才能声称DRL成功解决了机械臂避障任务。

Abstract: Deep reinforcement learning (DRL) has a longstanding tradition in addressing the reach-avoid task problem, especially for controlling robotic arms. While this task serves as a baseline environment within the research community, the ability of DRL to effectively learn the each-avoid task in complex and realistic scenarios beyond simplified and restricted tabletop settings remains uncertain. In this paper, we present, for the first time, a comprehensive benchmark for the reachavoid task that accurately captures real-world complexities without simplifications. We demonstrate a diverse range of settings for robotic arm reach-avoid task, which can be used for evaluating DRL research. We achieved this by utilizing the MuJoCo MJX physics engine and parallelizing both the simulation environment and DRL algorithms using the Brax library. We achieved state-of-the-art results with success rates of 96.1% (UR5e) and 98.8% (Franka Emika Robot) for the reach task and 86.8% (UR5e) and 95.2% (Franka) for the static reachavoid task. Our results indicate that while in previous works DRL agents could solve, for example, a reach task in a simplified setting perfectly, their agents performance collapses when evaluated in realistic scenarios. Overall, this work identifies that additional research is still required to claim the successful resolution of the robotic arm reach-avoid task using DRL. The environment and benchmarking code is available as open source at the following link

</details>


### [96] [A New Implementation of NeoSLAM and a Comparative Evaluation with RatSLAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16143)
*Joao Victor T. Borges,Fabio Coelho,Paulo Padrao,Jose Fuentes,Ramon R. Costa,Liu Hsu,Leonardo Bobadilla*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了基于ROS2模块化架构的NeoSLAM算法重写实现，通过现代框架实现实时执行并减少数据丢弃，在与RatSLAM的对比实验中展示了映射一致性和轨迹重建方面的有效性与可比性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有NeoSLAM实现缺乏模块化架构，无法充分利用现代框架实现实时执行，且缺少与RatSLAM的系统性对比评估。

Method: 将NeoSLAM完全重写为基于ROS2的模块化架构，采用现代框架实现实时执行并最小化输入数据丢弃；在三个不同环境条件下的数据集上进行对比评估。

Result: 新NeoSLAM在处理吞吐量上优于原版，满足实时应用需求；在映射重建方面与RatSLAM性能相当。

Conclusion: 提出的ROS2实现方案在保持与RatSLAM可比映射性能的同时，显著提升了NeoSLAM的实时处理能力，验证了其实际应用的有效性。

Abstract: This paper presents a new implementation of the NeoSLAM algorithm. The proposed version is a complete rewrite of NeoSLAM into a modular architecture using modern frameworks that, together, enable real-time execution with minimal discarding of input data. This work also provides a comparative evaluation between NeoSLAM and RatSLAM across three datasets under varying environmental conditions. The experimental results highlight differences in mapping consistency and trajectory reconstruction, demonstrating the effectiveness and practical applicability of the proposed ROS2-based implementation. The results indicate that the new NeoSLAM outperforms the original in terms of processing throughput for real-time applications and achieves comparable performance to RatSLAM in terms of map reconstruction across the evaluated datasets.

</details>


### [97] [VTLoc: Learning-based Tactile Contact Localization in Visual Point Clouds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16146)
*Zhiyuan Wu,Zhuo Chen,Shan Luo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VTLoc是一个视觉-触觉融合框架，通过几何多模态对齐模块和迭代定位更新器，利用3D点云作为视觉输入，实现从触觉读数中精确预测物体表面接触点位置。在100个真实物体数据集上，该方法显著降低了局部到全局的对应模糊性，提升了单点触觉接触定位精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉提供全局物体上下文，触觉提供接触点的精确局部信息，但二者的空间对齐存在挑战，难以准确预测触觉在物体表面的接触位置。

Method: 提出VTLoc框架，包含两个核心组件：1) 几何多模态对齐模块，从融合的视觉-触觉特征重建伪点云并与视觉点云对齐以强制执行空间一致性；2) 迭代定位更新器，通过融合的视觉-触觉特征迭代细化预测的接触位置。

Result: 在100个真实物体的新基准上，VTLoc改善了单点触觉接触定位性能，减少了局部到全局的对应模糊性。

Conclusion: 通过几何对齐和迭代更新的双重机制，VTLoc有效解决了视觉-触觉多模态融合中空间对齐难题，提升了接触定位的准确性。

Abstract: Vision and touch are complementary modalities essential for robotic perception and manipulation. While vision provides global object context, touch offers precise local information at contact points. Integrating these modalities for contact localization, i.e., predicting the location of touch on an object's surface, poses significant challenges due to the need for accurate spatial alignment between tactile data and visual geometry. To address this challenge, we propose VTLoc, a novel visual-tactile framework that localizes contact points from tactile readings using a 3D point cloud as visual input. VTLoc introduces two key components: a geometric multi-modal alignment module, which reconstructs a pseudo-point cloud from fused visual-tactile features and aligns it with the visual point cloud to enforce spatial consistencies across modalities; and an iterative localizing updater, which iteratively refines the predicted contact location using fused visual-tactile features. Evaluated on a new benchmark of 100 real-world objects, VTLoc improves single-touch contact localization by reducing local-to-global correspondence ambiguity.

</details>


### [98] [Vision-Language-Motion Maps: An Open-Vocabulary, Uncertainty-Aware, Queryable Motion Attribute for 3D Scene Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16173)
*Dibyendu Ghosh,Ayushi Shakya*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出VLMM（视觉-语言-运动地图），一种开放词汇、可自然语言查询的3D地图，其中每个场景元素携带融合的运动属性（语义先验+几何观测跨帧运动+逐元素不确定性），能够回答关于物体运动行为（是否在动、能否动、是否静止）的查询。实验表明各属性字段不可替代，不确定性通道有效提升运动检测精度，且后验校准可达到良好校准误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有开放词汇3D地图只能回答静态场景中"什么在哪"的问题，无法回答场景元素如何运动（如"什么在动"、"什么能动但没动"），亟需一种融合语义运动先验与几何观测运动、且支持自然语言查询的动态地图表示。

Method: VLMM为每个场景元素构建融合运动属性：1) VLM/LLM语义可动性先验（判断物体能否移动）；2) 几何跨帧运动观测（检测是否实际移动）；3) 逐元素不确定性度量。查询转化为属性过滤器，区分已观察到的运动、可能但未观察到的运动及静止物体。

Result: 在AI2-THOR模拟器基准上，消融实验证明语义+几何双通道不可或缺：纯语义基线无法回答"什么在动"，纯几何观测无法回答"什么能动"。在TUM/Bonn真实RGB-D序列上，不确定性通道持续提升运动/静态分类平均精度，减少假阳性；后验等渗校准使期望校准误差达0.10；对噪声位姿估计具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: VLMM是首个同时具备开放词汇、可语言查询、融合先验与观测运动、逐元素不确定性四项属性的3D地图表示，为机器人动态场景理解提供了新的表征基础。

Abstract: Open-vocabulary 3D maps let robots answer language queries about what and where, but they assume a static world and cannot answer queries about how scene elements behave. We introduce Vision-Language-Motion Maps (VLMM), an open-vocabulary, natural-language-queryable 3D map in which each element carries a fused motion attribute: a VLM/LLM semantic movability prior combined with geometrically observed cross-frame motion, together with a per-element uncertainty. Queries reduce to attribute filters that distinguish what has been seen to move, what could move but has not, and what stays still. On a controlled simulator benchmark with exact ground truth (AI2-THOR, three scene types) we show through ablation that the schema fields are non-substitutable: a semantic-only baseline fails motion queries even with strong features, and neither motion field substitutes for the other (the prior cannot answer "what is moving," observed motion cannot answer "what could move"). On real dynamic RGB-D (TUM and Bonn, six sequences) we show the uncertainty channel-our key difference from prior fused-motion work-consistently improves moving-vs-static average precision and reduces false motion flags, and is robust to estimated (noisy) poses. The raw confidence is not calibrated, but post-hoc isotonic calibration reaches an expected calibration error of 0.10. VLMM is a representation contribution: the closest prior maps each lack at least one of the four properties-open-vocabulary, language-queryable, fused prior-and-observed motion, and per-element uncertainty-that our combination provides.

</details>


### [99] [Handroid: Bridging Dexterous Hand and Humanoid](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16187)
*Ruogu Li,Chenyang Ma,Sikai Li,Zhenyu Wei,Yunchao Yao,Haochen Shi,C. Karen Liu,Shuran Song,Mingyu Ding*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Handroid是一个桌面级双形态机器人平台，通过重用同一套27自由度机电系统，灵活切换灵巧手（20自由度）和桌面人形（含头部、手臂和12自由度下肢）两种形态，支持操作、 locomotion、交互等多种任务，为形态可重构机器人学习提供了紧凑可复现的研究平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上，灵巧手和人形机器人被视为两种不同的实体——前者擅长物体尺度的接触丰富操作，后者提供人类环境中的移动和全身交互。作者希望将这两种能力整合到单一可重构平台中，以克服各自局限并实现更丰富的任务能力。

Method: 设计并制造了一个27自由度的机电本体，通过重新配置同一套模块化关节，可变形为两种形态：(1) 灵巧手形态：20个自由度构成拟人化手部；(2) 桌面人形形态：高度0.33米，重量2.05kg，包含头部、手臂和12自由度的下肢运动结构。同时提供统一的控制与学习框架，支持遥操作、抓取、手内操作、人形 locomotion、步态生成和交互式动作编排。

Result: 通过真实世界的灵巧操作实验、基于强化学习的locomotion、关键帧动作部署，以及一个包含形态重构、移动、对接和灵巧抓取放置的长时域任务，验证了平台的有效性。

Conclusion: Handroid是一个紧凑且可复现的平台，为推进形态可重构机器人和跨形态机器人学习提供了有力工具。

Abstract: Dexterous hands and humanoid robots are typically developed as distinct embodiments: the former enable contact-rich manipulation at the object scale, whereas the latter provide mobility and whole-body interaction in human-centered environments. We introduce \textbf{Handroid}, a desktop-scale dual-embodiment robot that integrates both capabilities within a single reconfigurable platform. Handroid reuses one 27-DoF electromechanical body as either a dexterous hand or a desktop humanoid, measuring 0.33 m in height and 2.05 kg in weight. In the dexterous hand embodiment, 20 DoFs form an anthropomorphic hand closely matching the kinematic structure of the human hand. In the humanoid embodiment, the same articulated modules are reconfigured into a humanoid with a head, arms, and legs, including a 12-DoF lower-limb structure for locomotion and whole-body motion. Handroid further provides a unified control and learning framework supporting hand teleoperation, dexterous grasping, in-hand manipulation, humanoid locomotion, gait generation, and interactive motion authoring. We validate the platform through real-world dexterous manipulation, reinforcement-learning-based locomotion, keyframe motion deployment, and a long-horizon task involving embodiment reconfiguration, locomotion, docking, and dexterous pick-and-place. These results position Handroid as a compact and reproducible platform for advancing morphology-reconfigurable robotics and cross-embodiment robot learning.

</details>


### [100] [Embodied Active Learning under Limited Annotation and Navigation Budget for Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15974)
*Hadrien Crassous,Mohamed Yassine Kabouri,Minahil Raza,Joni Pajarinen,Riad Akrour*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究在机器人导航时间和标注预算双重约束下，如何通过空间不一致性引导的体化批量主动学习策略，自适应地提升目标检测器在未知环境中的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在机器人导航场景中，预训练的物体检测器在未知环境中性能下降，而收集和标注新数据受限于导航时间和标注预算。现有主动学习方法多针对静态图像集，未考虑机器人导航的物理约束及空间连续性信息。

Method: 提出体化批量主动学习框架：每轮迭代中，智能体在有限导航预算下采集候选轨迹样本，并从其中选择最具信息量的图像进行标注。核心创新是利用空间一致性（spatial inconsistency）识别标签不一致的图像，作为检测器失败案例的代理信号，从而无外部监督地引导智能体选择高价值样本。

Result: 在AI2-THOR模拟器的大场景和波士顿动力Spot机器人+ YOLOv5的真实场景中，该方法在相同预算下达到最高的检测精度，优于多种基线方法。

Conclusion: 空间不一致性可作为有效的自我监督信号，在导航和标注预算约束下指导机器人主动收集和标注最有价值的训练样本，实现检测器的自适应提升。

Abstract: This paper studies how to adapt a computer vision object detector to an unknown environment under both a robot navigation time and annotation budget constraint. Our approach selects informative robot trajectories and image samples to retrain the detector, explicitly targeting its failure cases. Formally, the approach is an embodied variant of batch active learning, where at each round an agent has a limited navigation budget to collect candidate samples and a limited annotation budget for the most relevant images. We leverage spatial consistency to identify images with inconsistent labels, which are likely to provide the greatest improvement to the vision model. We evaluate the approach using different active learning objectives on large scenes from the AI2-THOR simulator and on a real-world setup using a Boston Dynamics Spot robot with the real-time object detector YOLOv5. Through comparison against several baselines, our experimental results show that spatial inconsistency helps guide the agent and select relevant images without external supervision, achieving the highest detection accuracy at the end of the adaptation process under the same budget. The open-source project can be found at https://mkabouri.github.io/embodied-active-learning-od

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [101] [GraphDx: A Cost-Aware Knowledge-Enhanced Multi-Agent Framework for Sequential Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15280)
*Shaoting Tan,Ning Liu,Yuntao Du,Shuyue Wei,Wu Shuai,Qian Li,Yanyu Xu,Wei Zhang,Lizhen Cui,Haitao Yuan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GraphDx是一个知识增强框架，通过构建医学诊断知识图谱（MDKG）和三个协作智能体（感知、推理、决策），将LLM的医学知识与成本感知的确定性推理相结合，在MedQA和MIMIC-IV上将诊断成功率从50-68%提升至79-93%，同时将测试成本降低20-54%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM方法存在知识-推理鸿沟：虽然编码了广泛的医学知识，但在成本约束下难以进行系统性推理，往往导致过度测试。

Method: GraphDx包含两个核心创新：1）自动化构建MDKG（医学诊断知识图谱），包含量化典型性、以行动为中心的双目标属性（诊断相关性和成本敏感性）；2）三个协作智能体——感知智能体和决策智能体处理语言理解和生成，推理智能体在MDKG上执行确定性证据评分和成本感知规划。

Result: 在MedQA和MIMIC-IV上，使用DeepSeek-V3、Kimi-k2、Llama-3.3三个LLM骨干，诊断成功率从50-68%提升至79-93%，测试成本降低20-54%。

Conclusion: GraphDx为自动化临床诊断提供了鲁棒、经济且可解释的解决方案。

Abstract: Sequential diagnosis requires balancing diagnostic accuracy against resource costs through iterative information gathering. Existing Large Language Model (LLM) approaches exhibit a critical knowledge-reasoning gap: despite encoding extensive medical knowledge, they struggle to reason systematically under cost constraints, often resorting to excessive testing. We propose GraphDx, a knowledge-enhanced framework with two core innovations. First, we design an automated pipeline that leverages LLMs to construct Medical Diagnosis Knowledge Graphs (MDKGs) with quantized typicality, action-centric topology, and dual-objective attributes for both diagnostic relevance and cost-sensitivity. Second, we introduce three collaborative agents (Perception, Reasoning, and Decision) where the Perception and Decision Agents handle language understanding and generation, while the Reasoning Agent performs deterministic evidence scoring and cost-aware planning on the MDKG. Experiments on MedQA and MIMIC-IV across three LLM backbones (DeepSeek-V3, Kimi-k2, Llama-3.3) show that GraphDx improves diagnostic success rates from 50--68% to 79--93% while reducing test costs by 20--54%, providing a robust, economical, and interpretable solution for automated clinical diagnosis.

</details>


### [102] [Causal-Audit: Explicit and Auditable Graph-based Reasoning via Target-Aware Causal Chain Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15281)
*Su Lan,Xuefei Yin,Yanming Zhu,Alan Wee-Chung Liew*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种显式且可审计的因果推理框架，用于无上下文干预型问答任务。该方法通过构建目标感知的因果图、模块化推理以及路径级因果证据聚合机制，在三个基准上持续优于现有LLM方法，并提供可解释的因果推理轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的方法在处理因果和干预性问题时依赖隐式语言推理，导致因果假设不透明、推理路径不可验证，且在复杂干预场景下预测脆弱，尤其缺乏对无上下文设置的有效支持。

Method: 提出显式因果推理框架，包含四个模块化阶段：1) 目标感知因果图构建，将目标变量作为核心约束抑制无关变量和虚假关系；2) 结构化因果推理；3) 路径级因果证据聚合，建模多重因果路径的强化与抵消效应；4) 基于聚合证据的决策输出。

Result: 在三个基准数据集上的实验表明，所提框架在性能上持续优于现有基于LLM的方法，同时能够提供可解释和可审计的因果推理轨迹。

Conclusion: 显式结构化因果推理比隐式端到端预测更适用于干预型问答，目标感知图构建和路径级证据聚合是关键创新，能有效提升LLM在因果推理任务中的鲁棒性和可解释性。

Abstract: Causal and intervention-based question answering is fundamental to advancing large language models (LLMs) toward reasoning beyond surface-level correlations and understanding underlying causal mechanisms. However, existing LLM-based methods often rely on implicit language-level reasoning, resulting in opaque causal assumptions, unverifiable reasoning paths, and fragile predictions under complex interventions, particularly in context-free settings. In this paper, we propose an explicit and auditable causal reasoning framework for context-free intervention-based question answering. Our method formulates causal inference as structured reasoning over an explicit causal graph through four modular stages, rather than implicit end-to-end prediction. A key innovation is a target-aware causal graph construction strategy that treats the target variable as a core constraint during graph expansion, effectively suppressing irrelevant variables, spurious causal relations, and reasoning noise. We further introduce a path-level causal evidence aggregation mechanism that combines multiple causal paths while modeling both reinforcing and counteracting effects, enabling robust decision-making beyond single-chain reasoning. Extensive experiments on three benchmarks demonstrate that our framework consistently outperforms existing LLM-based methods while providing interpretable and auditable causal reasoning traces.

</details>


### [103] [Cura 1T: Specialized Model for Agentic Healthcare](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15314)
*actAVA AI,:,Haolin Chen,Leon Qi,Steve Brown,Deon Metelski,Tao Xia,Joonyul Lee,Qixuan Wang,Kevin Riley,Frank Wang,Weiran Yao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Cura 1T 是一个通过人类门控的自我进化循环训练的医疗专用 LLM，通过迭代式数据优化在多任务医疗场景中达到前沿性能，同时保持通用推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗领域需要能同时处理患者咨询、临床推理、交互诊断和 EHR 工具使用等多种任务的专用 LLM，但现有模型要么覆盖不全面，要么某一任务的能力提升会损害其他任务的表现。

Method: 采用人类门控的自我进化循环，每轮进化中训练智能体规划目标能力、训练模型、评估基准轨迹、根据观察到的失败模式优化数据混合，通过定向合成和精选样本持续改进，而非单次通用医疗数据更新。

Result: 在医疗评估套件中排名领先或接近顶尖水平，同时在域外推理和智能体基准测试中保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 人类门控的自进化数据优化策略能有效训练多任务医疗 LLM，避免单一任务提升导致其他任务退化的问题。

Abstract: Healthcare spans high-stakes communication, expert reasoning, and workflow execution, yet specialized LLMs that cover these use cases together remain limited. A healthcare model must handle patient consultation, clinical reasoning over text and images, interactive diagnosis, and electronic health record (EHR) tool use. These capabilities fail in different ways, and a narrow update for one task can degrade another. We present Cura 1T, a healthcare-specialized LLM trained through a human-gated self-evolution loop. In each evolution round, a training agent plans a target capability, trains the model, evaluates benchmark trajectories, and refines the data mixture from observed failures. This data-centered loop improves the model through targeted synthetic and curated examples rather than a single generic medical-data update. Across the healthcare evaluation suite, Cura 1T ranks at or near the top among frontier baselines, while remaining competitive on out-of-domain reasoning and agentic benchmarks.

</details>


### [104] [AnovaX: A Local, Multi-Agent Voice Assistant with LLM Planning, Typed Executors, and Adaptive Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15367)
*Raunak B Sinha*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AnovaX 是一个完全运行在用户本地计算机上的桌面语音助手，通过单一 Python 进程集成了唤醒词检测、语音管道、LLM规划器、安全层、多智能体编排器与自适应恢复循环。它将桌面本身作为操作界面，所有工具映射为专有智能体类，支持递归元智能体与两级嵌套。配套Flask服务器通过本地WiFi提供手机端远程控制，并实时流式传输屏幕画面。整个项目仅数千行代码即可完成应用打开、文字输入、搜索、并发协调、单步失败恢复及手机远程操控，LLM全程不接触键盘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有桌面语音助手仍依赖云端管道将原始音频传输至远程服务器，暴露出固定的技能集合，缺乏灵活性和隐私保护。作者希望通过构建一个本地优先、轻量级且可扩展的开源框架，展示仅需数千行代码即可实现完整的桌面操控能力，而不必与商业巨头（Siri、Alexa）竞争。

Method: 单一 Python 进程集成以下组件：1. 唤醒词门控 → 语音管道（ASR）→ LLM规划器（Gemini，输出JSON格式工具调用计划）；2. 白名单-黑名单安全层过滤；3. 多智能体编排器将计划转化为有类型子智能体（AppAgent, TypingAgent, BrowserAgent等9种），各自含独立超时、重试策略和共享资源锁；4. 递归MetaAgent允许规划器将子目标回传给自己，最多两级嵌套；5. 自适应恢复循环使用紧凑的ReAct风格提示，通过只读工具的投机执行隐藏Gemini延迟；6. 配套Flask服务器通过本地WiFi提供手机端远程界面，实时镜像智能体生命周期事件并流式传输MJPEG屏幕画面。

Result: 成功实现了一个完全本地运行、数千行代码的桌面语音助手，能够完成打开应用、在应用中输入文字、运行搜索、协调并发操作、从单步失败中恢复，并完全通过手机远程操控，整个过程LLM不直接接触键盘。

Conclusion: AnovaX 证明了一个可读的、轻量级的本地优先架构足以覆盖桌面语音助手核心功能场景，无需依赖云端管道或庞大工程。其设计展示了LLM作为规划器与多智能体编排结合的可行路径，为未来隐私敏感、离线可用的桌面自动化工具提供了参考范式。

Abstract: Desktop voice assistants are still dominated by cloud pipelines that ship raw audio off the machine and expose a fixed set of skills. We describe AnovaX, a small local-first assistant that runs entirely on the user's computer and treats the desktop itself as its action surface. A single Python process wires together a wake-word gate, a speech pipeline, an LLM planner (Gemini) that emits a JSON plan of tool calls, a whitelist-and-denylist safety layer, a multi-agent orchestrator that translates each plan into typed child agents on a bounded thread pool, and an adaptive recovery loop that takes over whenever a core step fails. Every tool corresponds to a specialized agent class (AppAgent, TypingAgent, BrowserAgent and six others) with its own timeout, retry policy, and shared-resource locks. A recursive MetaAgent lets the planner delegate a sub-goal back to itself, capped at two levels of nesting. The recovery loop uses a compact ReAct-style prompt and hides Gemini's latency behind speculative execution of read-only tools. A companion Flask server exposes a phone-friendly remote over the local WiFi, mirrors every agent lifecycle event to the phone in real time, and streams the laptop's screen back over MJPEG so the user can watch remote commands land as they run. The point of the project is less to compete with Siri or Alexa than to show that a legible, few-thousand-line assistant is enough to open apps, type into them, run searches, coordinate concurrent actions, recover from single-step failures, and be driven entirely from a phone in another room -- without the LLM ever touching the keyboard.

</details>


### [105] [Precise but Uncoupled: Reviewer Precision Does Not Guarantee Critique Uptake in Multi-Agent Math Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15388)
*Chih-Hsuan Yang,Jingyan Jiang,Vikram Vasudevan,Cheng-Hau Yang,Huihuo Zheng,Le Chen,Eliu A. Huerta,Venkatram Vishwanath,Ian T. Foster,Rajeev Thakur*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对4,181个Omni-MATH问题进行了实证研究，发现广播式同行讨论在困难问题上的最终准确率高于规划-执行-审查流水线(PER)，原因是PER虽然审查者更精准(0.861 vs 0.644)，但其评审意见更难被采纳并转化为有效的答案修正，表明审查检测质量与批评采纳是分离的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验数学和科学领域智能体系统中常见的假设——专门的审查阶段应该能将错误候选方案转化为正确方案，探究不同协作协议（广播式讨论 vs. 规划-执行-审查流水线）的实际效果及其差异原因。

Method: 使用匹配的gpt-oss-120b模型作为actor，在4,181个经过验证器验证的Omni-MATH问题上进行实验，比较广播式同行讨论与PER流水线的性能，并通过分析审查者精度、批评采纳率、批评引导修复等指标解释性能差异。

Result: 广播式讨论在困难层级（tier 4及以上）的最终准确率高于PER；PER的审查者更精确(0.861 vs 0.644)，但其有用批评改变下一候选方案的可能性更低，产生的审查引导修复也较少；在PER干预中，强制显式确认会降低准确率，而将审查指导嵌入求解器工作上下文可部分改善跟进效果。

Conclusion: 以审查者为中心的评价可能高估系统质量：一个协议可能发现错误的能力很强，但如果不能将这些批评转化为实际的解答改进，仍然无法解决更多问题。审查检测质量与批评可采纳性是分离的。

Abstract: Many math- and science-oriented agent systems use hierarchical designs with specialized reviewer roles, assuming that a dedicated review stage should help turn wrong candidates into correct ones. We test this assumption on 4,181 verifier-grounded Omni-MATH problems using matched gpt-oss-120b actors. Collaboration adds little on the easiest tiers, but from tier 4 onward the gains open sharply; in this harder regime, broadcast-style peer discussion reaches higher final accuracy than a planner-executor-reviewer pipeline (PER). We ask whether this gap is explained by reviewer quality or by whether critique changes the next answer the protocol carries forward. It is not explained by reviewer precision alone: PER's reviewer is more precise than broadcast's (0.861 vs. 0.644), yet evaluator-verified useful critique is much less likely to change the next candidate and produces lower reviewer-guided repair. These results show that reviewer detection quality and critique uptake are empirically separable.
